Project function:
```
def project_sum_box(z, target):
    lo, hi = z.min()-1, z.max()+1
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(z-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    return np.clip(z-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
Because target within [0,n]. It returns exact sum ~ target within 1e-12; then adjust small residual by adding epsilon to non-boundary? Need sum exactly n/2 maybe for evaluation? The constraint sum(h)*dx=1; floating residual okay? But they might check? Better ensure sum close to target. After projection, compute diff=target-p.sum(); if diff nonzero, distribute to entries not at bounds (or split) until 0; due to bisection residual <=1e-12. We can do previous projection loops.

Alternative: Use exact bisection to set sum within 1e-12, then clip and adjust. Good.

Let's design CD precompute for a given sign s:
For p, d = s*(m-O). p as h: j=p+d valid; p as g: i=p-d valid.
Intervals:
- Interval H (p as h): j in [0,n-1] => d ∈ [-p, n-1-p]. m = O + s*d.
- Interval G (p as g): i in [0,n-1] => d ∈ [p-n+1, p]. m = O + s*d.
We can compute endpoints min/max.
For s=+1: H m [O-p, O+n-1-p] (decreasing? start=end? O-p to O+n-1-p if p small; for p=O maybe [0,2O]? G m [O+p-n+1, O+p] = [p-O? to O+p]. Union contiguous.
For s=-1: H m [O+p, O-(n-1-p)] (descending but range sorted [O-(N), O+p]). G m [O-p, O-(p-n+1)] = [O-p, O+n-1-p]. Union.
We can implement general loop over p:
```
mH1 = O + s*(-p); mH2 = O + s*(n-1-p); loH,hiH=sorted
mG1 = O + s*(p-n+1); mG2 = O + s*p; loG,hiG=sorted
lo=min(loH,loG); hi=max(hiH,hiG); ms=arange(lo,hi+1)
d = s*(ms-O)
jf = p + d; jb = p - d
precomp[p] = (lo,hi,ms,jf,jb)
```
This handles all.
Memory: store ms maybe not needed if we have lo,hi and compute d=jf=... on fly? Precompute jf,jb to avoid arithmetic. L total O(n^2). Use int16 for n<=32767. But lists of arrays overhead. Could store in Python list. For n=600, 600 arrays average 1200 =>720k each for jf,jb; 1.4M ints ~11MB, fine.

Exact coordinate delta function using precomp:
```
def solve_delta(base, vec, h0, U, iters=32):
    lo=-h0; hi=1-h0
    if lo==hi: return 0
    # quick evaluate at current 0? not needed
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       vals = base + mid*vec
       mval = vals.max()
       if mval <= U + 1e-12:
           # objective at U; we can try to find if can be below? impossible. choose mid? But maybe if all affected below U, any valid; mid okay.
           # However if U is not the global max? It is max unaffected; if affected below U, global U. This might be current max? Could be lower than old affected. good.
           return mid
       active = vals >= mval - 1e-10
       vmin = vec[active].min(); vmax = vec[active].max()
       if vmin > 1e-9:
           hi = mid
       elif vmax < -1e-9:
           lo = mid
       else:
           # 0 in subgradient of affected max; with U lower, min.
           return mid
    return (lo+hi)*0.5
```
But if U=-inf (all lags affected), setting mval <= U impossible; use U=-1. If all affected, objective only affected. The subgradient method still works. If no unaffected, U = -1e18. If active slopes mixed returns min. If all slopes positive -> hi, etc.
Now, this returns a delta that may increase c vs current? If current h already at local optimum, delta small ~0. We only apply if |delta|>tol. To ensure not worsen due numerical, we can compute new max and accept only if lower by >1e-12? But exact should not worsen; due U and clipping maybe. We can evaluate new global max before committing: affected new max + U. Also current global max? We can compute `new_max = max(U, (base+delta*vec).max())`; if new_max < c.max() - 1e-13 then update. But if delta=0 no. If new_max not lower, skip. This guard avoids errors.

Projection bisection:
```
def project_sum_box(z, n):
 target=0.5*n
 z=np.asarray(z,float)
 if z.size != n: ...
 lo = -1.0; hi = 1.0? Actually find theta s.t. sum(clip(z - theta,0,1))=target. As theta increases sum decreases.
 lo = z.min()+1? hi=z.max()-? Simpler: lo=min(z)-1.0, hi=max(z)+1.0
 for _ in range(50):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2
   s=np.clip(z-mid,0,1).sum()
   if s > target: lo=mid  # need smaller sum, increase theta
   else: hi=mid
 theta=(lo+hi)/2
 h=np.clip(z-theta,0,1)
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    # distribute among free
    free=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
    if free.sum(): h[free]+=diff/free.sum()
    else:
      # adjust all a little
      h+=diff/n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final tiny correction
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       idx=np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)); h[idx]=min(max(h[idx]+diff,0),1)
 return h
```
The inequality: If s > target, sum too high, need theta larger (z-theta smaller) -> lo=mid? We set theta low gives high sum. If s>target at mid, want increase theta, so lower bound lo=mid. Good. If s<target, hi=mid. Feasible target between sum at theta large (0) and small (n). Good.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-20
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)

 for n_small in [48,64,80,96]:

      if cbest < best_global: update

   if deadline close break
```

     if c1 < best: update
     new_pool.append((h1,n,c1,tag))
  # maybe also try parent resampled if not in pool
  pool = prune(new_pool, keep=8)
```

For p as complement: i=p-d => p-d valid; contribution -w_m h[p-d]. Here d = s*(m-O). So grad[p] = T1_s[p] - T2_s[p], where T1 = ∑ w(m) (1-h[p+d]), T2 = ∑ w(m) h[p-d]. We can compute with the same convolution formula using sequences W over d-values (not output m). But w defined over m; we need W(d) = weight[m] where m=O+s*d. Since s=±1, W_idx[d+s?]. For s=+1: W(d)=w[O+d]; for s=-1: W(d)=w[O-d]. So W over integer d is either w or reversed w. Then T1 is cross-correlation W(d) A(p+d). T2 cross-correlation W(d) H(p-d) = W_rev? We can use previous FFT formula with W(d) sequence and vector A, computing T1 = (W * A_rev)(-p). For T2 = ∑ W(d) H(p-d). This is -? We can express T2 = ∑ W(d) H(p-d). Let u=-d => -T1_formula with W(-u) H(p+u). So T2 = T1_conv(W2, H) where W2(u)=W(-u). But since W is from w and s, W2 maybe w? Let's implement generic using direct loops for gradient? For n up to 600, direct loop over m (1200) with slice additions might be acceptable for smooth optimizer if not too many iterations. Direct gradient:
```
def grad_from_weights(w,h,n,s):
 grad = np.zeros(n)
 for m in range(len(w)):
   wm = w[m]
   if wm <= 1e-15: continue
   d = s*(m-O)
   if d>=0:
      i=np.arange(0,n-d)
      grad[i] += wm * (1-h[i+d])
      grad[i+d] -= wm * h[i]
   else:
      i=np.arange(-d,n)
      j=i+d
      grad[i] += wm * (1-h[j])
      grad[i+d] -= wm * h[i]
 return grad
```
This is O(n*M) vector slice operations with M=2n-1; for n=600, ~1.2M slice additions per gradient, maybe 0.01-0.05s. If 1000 iterations, 10-50s. Acceptable? Projection and c each gradient O(n^2) but C-level. Could be okay. This avoids FFT complexity and sign bugs. We can also limit to top weights: Since weights from softmax; for large β only few active; for small β weights spread, can subsample? But smooth LSE gradient needs all weights. We can use active top K for steepest descent instead of full gradient. That is cheaper and similar to subgradient.

```

Now, exact CD vs active descent: exact CD processes coordinate by coordinate and can push to local optimum; active descent broad. Good combo.

Let's define `active_descent(h,n,iters,deadline,rng,topk=5,lr_init=0.05)` returns best.
Pseudo:
```
def active_descent(h,n,max_iter=2000,deadline,topk=5,lr=0.05):
 h=project; best=compute; cmax=...; gc=None
 stall=0
 for it in range(max_iter):
   if deadline and time> deadline: break
   if it%20==0 or gc is None:
      c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmax=c.max()
      # active indices: top cmax - 1e-7*cmax maybe; take up to topk highest, but if plateau include all within 1e-6 maybe limit
      order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
      active=order[:topk]
      # But if many near max, random shuffle among near: within tol take up to topk
      active = unique top maybe.
      gc=np.zeros(n)
      for m in active:
         d=s*(m-O)
         if d>=0: g=np.arange(0,n-d); gc[g]+=(1-h[g+d]); gc[g+d]-=h[g]
         else: ...
      gc /= len(active)
   # maybe add small random perturbation to gradient to escape
   z = h - lr*gc + rng.normal(0, lr*0.01, n) # tiny
   hp = project(z,n)
   cnew=compute
   if cnew < best - 1e-12:
      h=hp; best=cnew; stall=0; lr = min(0.25, lr*1.1)
   else:
      lr *= 0.75; stall +=1
      if lr < 1e-5 or stall>40: break
      if stall%10==0: gc=None
 return h,best
```

def cd_optimize(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None,shuffle=True,skip_inactive=True,active_tol=1e-8):
    h = project_sum_box(h,n).copy()
    dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    pre=get_precomp(n)
    length=2*n-1
    s? not needed pre includes contributions.
    for pass_i in range(max_passes):
       if deadline is not None and time.time()>deadline: break
       c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
       active = None
       if skip_inactive:
          cmax = float(c.max())
          tol = max(1e-10, active_tol*cmax)
          act = (c >= cmax - tol)
          prefix = np.zeros(length+1, dtype=np.int32)
          np.cumsum(act, out=prefix[1:])
       else: prefix=None
       order = np.arange(n)
       if shuffle and rng is not None: rng.shuffle(order)
       improved=False
       for p in order:
          if prefix is not None:
             item=pre[p]
             lo,hi,ifx,iju,ibx,jbu = item? Need order.
             if prefix[hi+1] - prefix[lo] == 0: continue
          ...
       if not improved: break
       h_proj=project_sum_box(h,n)
       c_proj_max = np.max(np.correlate(h_proj,1-h_proj,'full'))*dx
       if c_proj_max < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_c=c_proj_max; best_h=h_proj.copy()
       # For next pass, use h_proj (feasible)
       h = h_proj
       # maybe if deadline close break
    # after loop, project one more and evaluate; update if better
    h = project_sum_box(h,n)
    c = compute_c5(h,n)
    if c < best_c: best...
    return best_h,best_c
```

` solve_delta` function:
```
def solve_delta(base, vec, h0, U, iters=35):
    lo = -h0; hi = 1.0 - h0
    if lo == hi: return 0.0
    # optional if vec all zeros return 0
    for _ in range(iters):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       vals = base + mid*vec
       mval = float(vals.max())
       if mval <= U + 1e-11:
           return mid
       active = vals >= (mval - 1e-9)
       if not np.any(active): # no
           return mid
       vmin = float(vec[active].min())
       vmax = float(vec[active].max())
       if vmin > 1e-8:
           hi = mid
       elif vmax < -1e-8:
           lo = mid
       else:
           return mid
    return (lo+hi)*0.5
```
But if U = -1, `mval <= U+1e-11` false unless c negative; okay.
Tolerances: vec values O(1); 1e-8 okay. If slopes small, return mid; good.

The bisection logic returns when active slopes mixed, but what if active slopes mixed but 0 not in convex hull due U? U lower, slopes interval contains 0, yes. If active slopes all positive but an inactive line with negative slope will soon become max; bisection hi=mid moves left. Standard.

Could improve by after loop evaluating endpoints and mid to pick min to ensure not missing boundary:
```
cand=[(lo+hi)/2, lo, hi]
choose min of max(base+dvec, U)
```
But if lo/hi are current bounds from bisection not original endpoints? We lost original lo0/hi0. Store `lo0,hi0=lo,hi`; after loop evaluate `delta0, delta_mid, lo0, hi0`. But endpoint delta lo0=-h0 or hi0=1-h0 could be better if function monotone. Add:
```
def f_at(d): val=base+d*vec; return max(val.max(), U)
for d in [...]: if f_at(d)<best_f: best_d=d
return best_d
```
This is 5*O(L) per coordinate, maybe adds overhead 20%. Maybe do only if iters exhausted (not bisection returned early). Implement inside after loop. But if early return, fine.

Need ensure delta selection with `mval<=U` doesn't stop before finding a region where affected could go below U? As argued impossible to below U if U unaffected max. Good.

Now, active descent function with sign s:
```
def active_descent(h,n,max_iter,deadline,rng=None,topk=4,lr=0.05,noise=1e-4):
 h=project...
 best...
 c = correlate; pre? not needed.
 O=n-1; s=get_conv_sign(n)
 it=0; gc=None
 while it<max_iter and time<deadline:
   if gc is None or it%30==0:
     c = np.correlate(...)
     cmax=c.max(); tol=max(1e-8,1e-6*cmax)
     active = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
     if len(active)>topk:
        # include first (argmax) and random others
        amax=int(np.argmax(c))
        others=rng.choice(active, size=min(topk-1,len(active)-1), replace=False) if len>topk else active
        active=np.unique(np.concatenate(([amax], others)))
     grad=np.zeros(n)
     for m in active:
        d = s*(int(m)-O)
        if d>=0:
           grad[:n-d] += 1.0 - h[d:]
           grad[d:] -= h[:n-d]
        else:
           e=-d
           grad[e:] += 1.0 - h[:n-e]
           grad[:n-e] -= h[e:]
     if len(active)>0: grad /= len(active)
     gc=grad
   z = h - lr*gc
   if noise>0: z += rng.normal(0,noise,n)
   hp=project_sum_box(z,n)
   newc=compute_c5(hp,n)
   if newc < best - 1e-12:
      h=hp; best=newc; lr=min(0.2, lr*1.08); stall=0
   else:
      lr*=0.75; stall+=1
      if lr<1e-4 or stall>50: break
      if stall%10==0: gc=None
   it+=1
 return h,best
```
If active all many and topk=4, good. Use noise small 1e-4. This might wander. We can run limited.

`make_seed` uses `parent` optional. If parent passed different length, resample. It returns projected h.
```
def make_seed(n, kind, rng, parent=None):
    if parent is not None and kind == -1:
       return resample_area(parent,n)
    kind = int(kind) % 4
    if kind == 0:
       h=np.zeros(n)
       cnt=int(round(0.5*n))
       idx=rng.choice(n,cnt,replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
    elif kind == 1:
       x=2.0*(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
       a=rng.uniform(-2,2,3)
       b=rng.uniform(-2,2,3)
       h=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(np.pi*x*a + b) + 0.15*np.cos(2*np.pi*x*b)
    elif kind == 2:
       h=np.zeros(n)
       nb=rng.randint(2,5)
       bg=rng.uniform(0,0.3)
       h[:]=bg
       for _ in range(nb):
          start=int(rng.randint(0,n))
          length=int(rng.randint(1, max(1,n//4)))
          lo=start; hi=min(n,start+length)
          h[lo:hi]=1.0 # or random high
    else:
       h=0.5 + rng.uniform(-0.4,0.4,n)
    return project_sum_box(h,n)

`prune_pool` top-level:
```
def prune_pool(pool, keep):
    if len(pool) <= keep: return pool
    out=[]
    pool=list(pool)
    for _ in range(keep):
       minc=1e100; minidx=0
       for i in range(len(pool)):
          if pool[i][0] < minc:
             minc=pool[i][0]; minidx=i
       out.append(pool.pop(minidx))
    # maybe add one random? no rng.
    return out
```

```
def coord_effect(h, pre, p):
   lo,hi,fidx,fj,bidx,bj=pre[p]
   vec=np.zeros(hi-lo+1)
   if fidx.size: vec[fidx] += 1.0 - h[fj]
   if bidx.size: vec[bidx] -= h[bj]
   return lo,hi,vec
```
Used in cd and binary. Top-level no closure. Good.
`coord_effect` allocates zeros; in CD used per coordinate. In binary hill per move two calls. n small okay. For CD n=600, many calls; but helper overhead okay. Could inline for speed but helper clearer. The function not closure.
CD can use `coord_effect`.

def apply_coord(h,c,pre,p,delta):
   lo,hi,vec=coord_effect(h,pre,p)
   if np.any(vec): c[lo:hi+1] += delta*vec
   h[p] = float(np.clip(h[p]+delta,0,1))

```
def binary_hill(h,n,moves,deadline,rng):
   h=np.where(np.round(h)>0.5,1.0,0.0)
   target=n/2; # adjust
   while target - h.sum() > 0: pick zero set 1
   while h.sum() - target > 0: pick one set 0
   pre=get_precomp(n)
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.max()*(2/n)
   for it in range(moves):
      if deadline and time>deadline: break
      ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=...
      if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
      p1=int(rng.choice(ones)); p2=int(rng.choice(zeros))
      # evaluate new max efficiently
      newc=c.copy()
      apply coord p1 -1; p2 +1? But apply modifies newc and h? We can make copy htoo? Too expensive. Instead compute vector effects using current h, combine manually:
         lo1,hi1,vec1=coord_effect(h,pre,p1)
         lo2,hi2,vec2=coord_effect(h,pre,p2)
         newc=c.copy()
         if vec1 nonzero: newc[lo1:hi1+1] += -vec1
         if vec2 nonzero: newc[lo2:hi2+1] += vec2
         if newc.max() < cmax - 1e-12: accept: c=newc; h[p1]=0; h[p2]=1; best update
      # if no accept, maybe continue
   h=best_h; cmax? return project? Binary sum exact; project no change.
```
When p1/p2 effects overlap, adding both to newc works because the bilinear cross effect between p1 and p2? Changing two variables simultaneously, c_m as function quadratic in delta1,delta2: c = c0 + delta1*g1 + delta2*g2 + delta1*delta2*interaction. For point changes (0/1), g1/g2 computed with current h includes the other variable current value; when applying sequentially, second update should account for first change if they interact in same c_m. Are there interactions? c_m = ∑ h_i - h_i h_j. If changing p1 and p2, there is a product term h_{p1}h_{p2} if p2=p1±d for some m. Then c_m quadratic: g1 includes -h_{p2} (current 0), g2 includes -h_{p1} (current 1). Adding -vec1 + vec2 with current h: Let's test swap p1 (1->0), p2 (0->1) in a lag where they are paired. Original product 0. New product 0 (since now p1=0,p2=1) if pair h_p1(1-h_p2) original: h1=1, h2=0 => term0; new h1=0,h2=1 => term0. Net 0. vec1 for p1 delta -1: coefficient = (1-h2)=1 => -1. vec2 for p2 delta +1: coefficient = -? p2 as complement? If p2 paired as complement, coefficient -h1=-1; delta +1 gives -1. Sum -2, wrong. Let's define p1=1,p2=0 and pair h_p1*(1-h_p2) term original 1; after p1=0,p2=1 term 0. So net -1. If apply p1 first: delta -1 coefficient 1-h2_old=1 => c decreases by1. Then p2 with h1_new=0 coefficient 0 => no change. Net -1 correct. Precomputing both with current and adding gives -2 because it double counts. Thus need sequential with updated h or compute quadratic interaction. For smooth seeds, skip binary hill or threshold? Not.

Maybe also apply binary hill to parent downsampled (threshold) for small n.

Now, further thought: Since d=0 pushes binary, maybe optimal continuous with values 0/1/0.5? A hybrid. CD covers.

Let's test one candidate concept: maybe h is indicator of a binary sequence from a 1D spin glass. Our low-res search may find.

Now, code for `run` must track time. We'll use `time.time()` a few times. Ensure `deadline` not exceeded. In CD, if a pass takes long, it checks at start of pass only, maybe overrun. Fine.

Potential high time in CD final: Use deadlines with margin.

Let's write code carefully.

Detailed function signatures:
```
def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
```
Could optimize by using `c = np.correlate...` and `np.max`. Good.

`project_sum_box` includes final exact adjustment:
```
def project_sum_box(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    if z.size != n:
       # handle by resample? just clip and length? But should not.
       z = np.resize(z,n)
    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(z)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       s = float(np.clip(z - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
       if s > target:
          lo = mid
       else:
          hi = mid
    theta = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    h = np.clip(z - theta, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
       diff = target - float(h.sum())
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: break
       free = np.where((h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8))[0]
       if free.size:
           h[free] += diff / free.size
       else:
           idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
           h[idx] += diff
       np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
When `free.size` and diff/free.size may push some out of bounds, clip; loop again. If all at bounds and diff not representable, adjust one may go out; but target feasible, should be small.

`get_sign`:
```
def get_sign(n):
    if n in _SIGN_CACHE: return _SIGN_CACHE[n]
    if n <= 1: s=1
    else:
       e0=np.zeros(n); e0[0]=1.0
       c=np.correlate(e0,1.0-e0,mode='full')
       s = -1 if c[0] >= c[-1] else 1
    _SIGN_CACHE[n]=s
    return s
```
Cache dict globally. If memory, small.

`get_precomp`:
```
def get_precomp(n):
    if n in _PRECOMP: return _PRECOMP[n]
    if len(_PRECOMP) >= 8: _PRECOMP.clear()
    s=get_sign(n); O=n-1; pre=[]
    for p in range(n):
      mH1=O+s*(-p); mH2=O+s*(n-1-p)
      mG1=O+s*(p-(n-1)); mG2=O+s*p
      lo1,hi1=sorted((mH1,mH2)); lo2,hi2=sorted((mG1,mG2))
      lo=min(lo1,lo2); hi=max(hi1,hi2)
      ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1,dtype=np.int64)
      d=s*(ms-O)
      jf=p+d; jb=p-d
      idx=np.arange(len(ms),dtype=np.int32)
      vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
      fidx=idx[vf].astype(np.int32); fj=jf[vf].astype(np.int32)
      bidx=idx[vb].astype(np.int32); jbv=jb[vb].astype(np.int32)
      pre.append((lo,hi,fidx,fj,bidx,jbv))
    _PRECOMP[n]=pre
    return pre
```
Need sorted of ints; Python sorted returns list, okay. Could use min/max more efficient:
```
lo1,hi1 = (mH1,mH2) if mH1<=mH2 else (mH2,mH1)
```
Do that.
`p-(n-1)` correct: p-n+1.

`coord_effect(h, pre, p)`:
```
lo,hi,fidx,fj,bidx,bj = pre[p]
vec = np.zeros(hi-lo+1, dtype=float)
if fidx.size: vec[fidx] += 1.0 - h[fj]
if bidx.size: vec[bidx] -= h[bj]
return lo,hi,vec
```
`h` is 1d float.

`apply_coord(h,c,pre,p,delta)`:
```
lo,hi,vec=coord_effect(h,pre,p)
if vec.size:
   c[lo:hi+1] += delta*vec
h[p] = float(np.clip(h[p]+delta,0.0,1.0))
```
In cd, we don't want to apply unless accepted; we use coord_effect.

`solve_delta` as above; to avoid repeated allocations `vals = base + mid*vec`. Good.

`cd_optimize` uses `apply`? It computes vec via coord_effect. To avoid storing pre in variable item each time? okay.
Potential issue: In CD, c array updated in place; after project recompute new c. The precomp vec depends on h; in loop after updating previous h, subsequent p uses updated h. Good.

`cd_optimize` code detail:
```
def cd_optimize(h, n, max_passes, deadline=None, rng=None, shuffle=True, skip_inactive=True, active_tol=1e-6):
    h = project_sum_box(h,n).copy()
    dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    pre=get_precomp(n)
    L=2*n-1
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline: break
       c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       prefix=None
       if skip_inactive:
          cmax=float(c.max())
          tol=max(1e-9, active_tol*cmax)
          act = c >= (cmax - tol)
          prefix = np.empty(L+1, dtype=np.int32)
          prefix[0]=0
          np.cumsum(act.astype(np.int32), out=prefix[1:])
       order=np.arange(n)
       if shuffle and rng is not None: rng.shuffle(order)
       improved=False
       for p in order:
          lo,hi,fidx,fj,bidx,bj=pre[p]
          if prefix is not None and prefix[hi+1] == prefix[lo]: continue
          h0=float(h[p])
          # even if h0 0 or 1 allow
          vec=np.zeros(hi-lo+1)
          if fidx.size: vec[fidx] += 1.0 - h[fj]
          if bidx.size: vec[bidx] -= h[bj]
          # if no nonzero, skip
          if not (vec.size and float(abs(vec).sum()) > 1e-15): continue? abs sum expensive. Could just check fidx/bidx empty? but vec could cancel. We can skip if fidx.size==0 and bidx.size==0 (impossible maybe). Use if not fidx.size and not bidx.size.
          base=c[lo:hi+1]
          U=-1.0
          if lo>0:
             ml=float(c[:lo].max()); U=max(U,ml)
          if hi+1<L:
             mr=float(c[hi+1:].max()); U=max(U,mr)
          delta=solve_delta(base,vec,h0,U)
          if abs(delta) <= 1e-13: continue
          new_chunk = base + delta*vec
          new_max = float(new_chunk.max())
          if U > new_max: new_max = U
          old_max = float(base.max())
          if U > old_max: old_max = U
          if new_max < old_max - 1e-13:
             c[lo:hi+1] = new_chunk
             h[p] = float(np.clip(h0+delta,0.0,1.0))
             improved=True
       if not improved:
          break
       h = project_sum_box(h,n)
       cc = compute_c5(h,n)
       if cc < best_c:
          best_c=cc; best_h=h.copy()
       # continue with projected h
    h=project_sum_box(h,n)
    cc=compute_c5(h,n)
    if cc < best_c: best...
    return best_h,best_c
```
If `prefix[hi+1] == prefix[lo]` skip. But if prefix dtype int32 and hi+1 index okay. `act.astype(np.int32)` may copy; length 2000, okay. Could use `np.cumsum(act, out=prefix[1:], dtype=np.int32)`? `out` dtype int32 and act bool casts. Fine.
`vec.size` always >0. If fidx,bidx empty? For p maybe not. skip if both empty impossible because at least d=0 both.

Potential issue: Solving delta with `base.max()` in accept; if base empty? no. If U=-1 and base max negligible? okay.

`active_descent` top-level:
```
def active_descent(h,n,max_iter=1000,deadline=None,rng=None,topk=4,lr=0.05,noise=1e-4):
 h=project_sum_box(h,n).copy()
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
 s=get_sign(n); O=n-1; gc=None; stall=0; it=0
 while it<max_iter:
  if deadline and time>deadline: break
  if gc is None or it%40==0:
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); cmax=float(c.max()); tol=max(1e-8,1e-6*cmax)
    active=np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
    if active.size ==0: active=np.array([O],dtype=int)
    if active.size > topk:
       amax=int(np.argmax(c))
       if rng is not None and active.size-1 >= topk-1:
          others=rng.choice(active, size=topk-1, replace=False)
       else:
          others=active[:max(0,topk-1)]
       active=np.unique(np.concatenate(([amax], others)))
       if active.size>topk: active=active[:topk]
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for m in active:
       d=s*(int(m)-O)
       if d >= 0:
          grad[:n-d] += 1.0 - h[d:]
          grad[d:] -= h[:n-d]
       else:
          e=-d
          grad[e:] += 1.0 - h[:n-e]
          grad[:n-e] -= h[e:]
    if active.size>1: grad = grad / float(active.size)
    gc=grad
  z = h - lr*gc
  if noise > 0 and rng is not None: z = z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
  hp=project_sum_box(z,n)
  nc=compute_c5(hp,n)
  if nc < best_c - 1e-13:
    h=hp; best_c=nc; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0; lr=min(0.25, lr*1.05)
  else:
    lr *= 0.75; stall +=1
    if lr < 1e-5 or stall > 50: break
    if stall % 10 == 0: gc=None
  it +=1
 return best_h,best_c
```
If d=0, slices `grad[:n] += 1-h`, `grad[0:] -=h`, okay. For d>0, `n-d` positive. For d negative, e positive.
If active selection uses `rng.choice(active, size=topk-1, replace=False)`, active contains np ints; okay. If `topk=1`, others size0; `active[:0]` and concat [amax]. Good.
This function recomputes C every iteration (np.correlate O(n^2)). For n=600 and 2000 iter, 2000*360k=720M C, maybe heavy but acceptable? 2000 maybe too much. Limit max_iter. At final 500 maybe. We'll use modest.

`binary_hill` top-level:
```
def binary_hill(h,n,iterations=2000,deadline=None,rng=None):
    h = np.where(np.round(h) > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).copy()
    target = 0.5*n
    # adjust sum
    ones=np.nonzero(h)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(1-h)[0]
    while h.sum() < target - 1e-12:
       if zeros.size==0: break
       idx=int(rng.choice(zeros)); h[idx]=1.0; zeros=np.nonzero(1-h)[0]
    while h.sum() > target + 1e-12:
       if ones.size==0: break
       idx=int(rng.choice(ones)); h[idx]=0.0; ones=np.nonzero(h)[0]
    pre=get_precomp(n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full').copy()
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=float(c.max())*(2.0/n)
    current_cmax=float(c.max())
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(iterations):
       if deadline and time.time()>deadline: break
       ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
       if ones.size==0 or zeros.size==0: break
       p1=int(rng.choice(ones)); p2=int(rng.choice(zeros))
       newh=h.copy(); newc=c.copy()
       apply_coord(newh,newc,pre,p1,-1.0)
       apply_coord(newh,newc,pre,p2,1.0)
       newmax=float(newc.max())
       if newmax < current_cmax - 1e-12:
          h=newh; c=newc; current_cmax=newmax
          cc=newmax*(2.0/n)
          if cc < best_c: best_c=cc; best_h=h.copy()
          no_improve=0
       elif newmax <= current_cmax + 1e-12 and rng.rand() < 0.15:
          h=newh; c=newc; current_cmax=newmax
          no_improve +=1
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 2000: break
       # optional: if many no improve, maybe try a few extra random moves? break
    return best_h,best_c
```
The plateau accept copies arrays; probability 0.15. If h new equal cmax, no_improve increases; could eventually break. Good.
Note `current_cmax` updates on plateau; best separate.
Could get stuck in local minima; random restarts via different seeds.

`resample_area`:
```
def resample_area(h, n_dest):
    n_src=len(h)
    if n_dest == n_src:
       return project_sum_box(h,n_dest)
    dxo=2.0/n_src
    area=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(h)*dxo))
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_src+1)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_dest+1)
    area_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, area)
    hnew=(area_new[1:]-area_new[:-1])/(2.0/n_dest)
    return project_sum_box(hnew,n_dest)
```
Area cumulative: `np.cumsum(h)*dxo` starts at first sample. `np.interp` with x_old linear. Good. If h has tiny out of bounds, interp within. Project.

`mix_optimize` top-level:
```
def mix_optimize(ha, hb, n, max_iters=35):
    ha=project_sum_box(ha,n)
    hb=project_sum_box(hb,n)
    gr=(np.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    lo=0.0; hi=1.0
    best_alpha=0.5
    # initialize? Golden needs two evals per iter; do loop, keep best
    best_c=compute_c5(0.5*ha+0.5*hb,n)
    best_h=project_sum_box(0.5*ha+0.5*hb,n)
    for _ in range(max_iters):
       x1=lo + (1.0-gr)*(hi-lo)
       x2=lo + gr*(hi-lo)
       h1=x1*ha+(1-x1)*hb; h1=project_sum_box(h1,n); c1=compute_c5(h1,n)
       h2=x2*ha+(1-x2)*hb; h2=project_sum_box(h2,n); c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c1<best_c: best_c=c1; best_alpha=x1; best_h=h1.copy()
       if c2<best_c: best_c=c2; best_alpha=x2; best_h=h2.copy()
       if c1 < c2:
          hi = x2
       else:
          lo = x1
    h=best_alpha*ha+(1-best_alpha)*hb
    h=project_sum_box(h,n)
    c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
It projects many times; convex combination of feasible with same sum => sum target; project cheap but maybe unnecessary. Since both sum target and bounds, convex is feasible; we can skip project inside except final? Sum exactly if linear: target*n? Since sum(a)=sum(b)=target, sum(xa+(1-x)b)=target. Bounds [0,1]. So skip project for evals to speed. But numerical tiny. Use direct. Final project for exact. In eval, `h1 = x1*ha + (1-x1)*hb`; compute. At end project.
```
best_h=0.5*ha+0.5*hb; best_c=compute...
loop no project inside
final h=...; h=project; c=...
```
Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       parent = np.full(600, 0.5)
    if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size < 2:
       parent = np.full(600,0.5)
    parent = project_sum_box(parent, parent.size)
    pool=[]
    best_h = parent.copy(); best_c = 1.0
    # consider parent and variants
    h, c, best_h, best_c = consider(parent, pool, best_h, best_c, 'parent')
    if parent.size > 1:
       h, c, best_h, best_c = consider(parent[::-1], pool, best_h, best_c, 'rev')
       hs = 0.5*(parent + parent[::-1])
       h, c, best_h, best_c = consider(hs, pool, best_h, best_c, 'sym')
    # quick CD on best
    parent_deadline = t0 + max(30.0, 0.10*total)
    h_cd, c_cd = cd_optimize(best_h, len(best_h), 150, deadline=parent_deadline, rng=rng, shuffle=True, skip_inactive=True)
    h_cd, c_cd, best_h, best_c = consider(h_cd, pool, best_h, best_c, 'parent_cd')
    # low-res
    low_deadline = t0 + 0.30*total
    low_ns = [32, 40, 48, 64, 80, 104, 128, 160]
    seed_offset = seed % 7
    for n_small in low_ns:
        if time.time() > low_deadline: break
        for k in range(3):
            if time.time() > low_deadline: break
            if k == 0:
                hseed = resample_area(best_h, n_small)
                tag = 'ps'+str(n_small)
            else:
                hseed = make_seed(n_small, (k + seed_offset + n_small) % 4, rng, best_h)
                tag = 'rd'+str(n_small)+'_'+str(k)
            # binary hill for k maybe all
            hh, cc = binary_hill(hseed, n_small, iterations=max(500, 20000//n_small), deadline=low_deadline, rng=rng)
            # CD
            passes = max(40, min(350, int(18000 // max(1, n_small))))
            hbest, cval = cd_optimize(hh, n_small, passes, deadline=low_deadline, rng=rng, shuffle=True, skip_inactive=True)
            hbest, cval, best_h, best_c = consider(hbest, pool, best_h, best_c, tag+'_cd')
            # active
            if time.time() < low_deadline:
               hact,cact=active_descent(hbest,n_small,max_iter=min(400, 20000//max(1,n_small)),deadline=low_deadline,rng=rng,topk=3,lr=0.04)
               hact,cact,best_h,best_c=consider(hact,pool,best_h,best_c,tag+'_ad')
            pool=prune_pool(pool,12)
```

       hm,cm,best_h,best_c = consider(hm,pool,best_h,best_c,'mix_'+tag_in)
       # quick CD after mix
       if time.time() < deadline - 40:
          hcd,ccd=cd_optimize(hm,n_mix,100,deadline=deadline-20,rng,rng,shuffle=True,skip_inactive=True)
          consider...
```

import numpy as np, time
_PRECOMP={}; _SIGN={}
def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*2.0/n)
def project_sum_box(z,n):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).copy(); t=0.5*n
    if z.size!=n: z=np.resize(z,n)
    lo=float(z.min())-1; hi=float(z.max())+1
    for _ in range(50):
        m=.5*(lo+hi); s=float(np.clip(z-m,0,1).sum())
        if s>t: lo=m
        else: hi=m
    h=np.clip(z-.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    for _ in range(5):
        d=t-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-13: break
        f=np.where((h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9))[0]
        if f.size: h[f]+=d/f.size
        else:
            i=int(np.argmin(abs(h-.5))); h[i]+=d
        np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
...
```

```
def cd_optimize(h,n,passes,deadline=None,rng=None,shuffle=True,skip=True,tol=1e-6):
    h=project_sum_box(h,n).copy(); pre=get_precomp(n); L=2*n-1
    bh=h.copy(); bc=compute_c5(h,n)
    for _ in range(passes):
        if deadline and time.time()>deadline: break
        c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); pref=None
        if skip:
            cm=float(c.max()); a=c>=cm-tol*max(1.0,cm)
            pref=np.empty(L+1,np.int32); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(a,out=pref[1:])
        order=np.arange(n)
        if shuffle and rng is not None: rng.shuffle(order)
        imp=False
        for p in order:
            lo,hi,fi,fj,bi,bj=pre[p]
            if pref is not None and pref[hi+1]==pref[lo]: continue
            vec=np.zeros(hi-lo+1)
            if fi.size: vec[fi]+=1-h[fj]
            if bi.size: vec[bi]-=h[bj]
            if not fi.size and not bi.size: continue
            h0=h[p]; base=c[lo:hi+1]; U=-1.0
            if lo: U=max(U,c[:lo].max())
            if hi+1<L: U=max(U,c[hi+1:].max())
            d=solve_delta(base,vec,h0,U)
            if abs(d)>1e-13:
                new=base+d*vec; nf=float(new.max()); o=U*0? 
                nm=nf if nf>U else U; om=float(base.max()); om=max(om,U)
                if nm<om-1e-11:
                    c[lo:hi+1]=new; h[p]=float(np.clip(h0+d,0,1)); imp=True
        if not imp: break
        h=project_sum_box(h,n); cc=compute_c5(h,n)
        if cc<bc: bc=cc; bh=h.copy()
    h=project_sum_box(h,n); cc=compute_c5(h,n)
    if cc<bc: bc=cc; bh=h.copy()
    return bh,bc
```
Need `c[:lo].max()` returns np float; `max(U, ...)` where U maybe -1 works. If slice empty no due if.
`if not fi.size and not bi.size` skip; but if both empty vec zeros. Good.
Note `vec[bi]-=h[bj]` uses `h` float; okay.
`pref` cumsum: `np.cumsum(a, out=pref[1:])` where a bool, out int32; works? `np.cumsum` accepts out dtype maybe; bool to int32? It should. If not, use `np.cumsum(a.astype(np.int32), out=pref[1:])` but alloc. Maybe `pref[1:] = np.cumsum(a)` (temp bool to int64). For small okay. Use `pref[1:] = np.cumsum(a)`.

`active_descent` compact:
```
def active_descent(h,n,iters,deadline=None,rng=None,topk=4,lr=.05,noise=1e-4):
    h=project_sum_box(h,n).copy(); bh=h.copy(); bc=compute_c5(h,n)
    s=get_sign(n); O=n-1; g=None; stall=0; it=0
    while it<iters:
        if deadline and time.time()>deadline: break
        if g is None or it%40==0:
            c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cm=float(c.max()); a=np.where(c>=cm-max(1e-8,1e-6*cm))[0]
            if a.size>topk:
                am=int(np.argmax(c))
                if rng is not None: o=rng.choice(a,min(topk-1,a.size-1),replace=False)
                else: o=a[:max(0,topk-1)]
                a=np.unique(np.concatenate(([am],o)))
            gp=np.zeros(n)
            for m in a:
                d=s*(int(m)-O)
                if d>=0:
                    gp[:n-d]+=1-h[d:]; gp[d:]-=h[:n-d]
                else:
                    e=-d; gp[e:]+=1-h[:n-e]; gp[:n-e]-=h[e:]
            if a.size>1: gp/=a.size
            g=gp
        z=h-lr*g
        if noise and rng is not None: z += rng.normal(0,noise,n)
        hp=project_sum_box(z,n); nc=compute_c5(hp,n)
        if nc<bc-1e-13: h=hp; bh=h.copy(); bc=nc; stall=0; lr=min(.25,lr*1.05)
        else:
            lr*=.75; stall+=1
            if lr<1e-5 or stall>50: break
            if stall%10==0: g=None
        it+=1
    return bh,bc
```
`np.where` returns array; if a empty no. `d` can be 0; `n-d` n; `gp[:n]+=...` okay. If d positive n-d. If d negative, e. Good.
`rng.choice(a, min(topk-1,a.size-1), replace=False)`: if size 0? topk-1 maybe 0; choice with size 0 returns empty? It might error if size 0? `rng.choice(np.array([...]),0)` returns empty; okay. But topk maybe 1 and a.size-1=0, min 0. Good. If rng not None but a.size-1 < size? min.

```
def apply_coord(h,c,pre,p,d):
    lo,hi,fi,fj,bi,bj=pre[p]
    vec=np.zeros(hi-lo+1)
    if fi.size: vec[fi]+=1-h[fj]
    if bi.size: vec[bi]-=h[bj]
    if fi.size or bi.size: c[lo:hi+1]+=d*vec
    h[p]=float(np.clip(h[p]+d,0,1))
```

Could be too narrow; missing lags close but not max. Use active_tol=1e-5 -> cm*1e-5=0.001 for c=100; okay. Use 1e-5.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_PRECOMP = {}
_SIGN_CACHE = {}

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * 2.0 / n)

def project_sum_box(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    if z.size != n:
        z = np.resize(z, n)
    t = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(z)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(z - m, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > t:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    h = np.clip(z - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(6):
        d = t - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(d) <= 1e-13:
            break
        free = np.where((h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9))[0]
        if free.size:
            h[free] += d / free.size
        else:
            i = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
            h[i] += d
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h

def get_sign(n):
    if n in _SIGN_CACHE:
        return _SIGN_CACHE[n]
    if n <= 1:
        s = 1
    else:
        e0 = np.zeros(n)
        e0[0] = 1.0
        c = np.correlate(e0, 1.0 - e0, mode='full')
        s = -1 if c[0] >= c[-1] else 1
    _SIGN_CACHE[n] = s
    return s

def get_precomp(n):
    if n in _PRECOMP:
        return _PRECOMP[n]
    if len(_PRECOMP) >= 8:
        _PRECOMP.clear()
    s = get_sign(n)
    O = n - 1
    pre = []
    for p in range(n):
        a1 = O + s * (-p)
        a2 = O + s * (n - 1 - p)
        b1 = O + s * (p - (n - 1))
        b2 = O + s * p
        lo1, hi1 = (a1, a2) if a1 <= a2 else (a2, a1)
        lo2, hi2 = (b1, b2) if b1 <= b2 else (b2, b1)
        lo = min(lo1, lo2)
        hi = max(hi1, hi2)
        ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        d = s * (ms - O)
        jf = p + d
        jb = p - d
        idx = np.arange(len(ms), dtype=np.int32)
        vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
        vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
        pre.append((lo, hi, idx[vf].astype(np.int32), jf[vf].astype(np.int32),
                    idx[vb].astype(np.int32), jb[vb].astype(np.int32)))
    _PRECOMP[n] = pre
    return pre

def coord_effect(h, pre, p):
    lo, hi, fi, fj, bi, bj = pre[p]
    vec = np.zeros(hi - lo + 1, dtype=float)
    if fi.size:
        vec[fi] += 1.0 - h[fj]
    if bi.size:
        vec[bi] -= h[bj]
    return lo, hi, vec

def apply_coord(h, c, pre, p, delta):
    lo, hi, vec = coord_effect(h, pre, p)
    if vec.size:
        c[lo:hi + 1] += delta * vec
    h[p] = float(np.clip(h[p] + delta, 0.0, 1.0))

def solve_delta(base, vec, h0, U, iters=34):
    lo = -h0
    hi = 1.0 - h0
    if lo == hi:
        return 0.0
    lo0, hi0 = lo, hi
    for _ in range(iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        vals = base + mid * vec
        mv = float(np.max(vals))
        if mv <= U + 1e-11:
            return mid
        at = mv - max(1e-7, 1e-8 * abs(mv))
        act = vals >= at
        if not np.any(act):
            return mid
        vmn = float(np.min(vec[act]))
        vmx = float(np.max(vec[act]))
        if vmn > 1e-8:
            hi = mid
        elif vmx < -1e-8:
            lo = mid
        else:
            return mid
    bd = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    bf = float(np.max(base + bd * vec))
    if U > bf:
        bf = U
    for d in (lo0, hi0):
        f = float(np.max(base + d * vec))
        if U > f:
            f = U
        if f < bf:
            bf = f
            bd = d
    return bd

def cd_optimize(h, n, passes, deadline=None, rng=None, shuffle=True, skip=True, act_tol=1e-5):
    h = project_sum_box(h, n).copy()
    pre = get_precomp(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    bh = h.copy()
    bc = compute_c5(h, n)
    for _ in range(passes):
        if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        pref = None
        if skip:
            cm = float(np.max(c))
            a = c >= cm - act_tol * max(1.0, cm)
            pref = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.int32)
            pref[0] = 0
            pref[1:] = np.cumsum(a)
        order = np.arange(n)
        if shuffle and rng is not None:
            rng.shuffle(order)
        improved = False
        for p in order:
            lo, hi, fi, fj, bi, bj = pre[p]
            if pref is not None and pref[hi + 1] == pref[lo]:
                continue
            if not fi.size and not bi.size:
                continue
            vec = np.zeros(hi - lo + 1, dtype=float)
            if fi.size:
                vec[fi] += 1.0 - h[fj]
            if bi.size:
                vec[bi] -= h[bj]
            h0 = float(h[p])
            base = c[lo:hi + 1]
            U = -1.0
            if lo > 0:
                U = max(U, float(np.max(c[:lo])))
            if hi + 1 < L:
                U = max(U, float(np.max(c[hi + 1:])))
            d = solve_delta(base, vec, h0, U)
            if abs(d) > 1e-13:
                newc = base + d * vec
                nnf = float(np.max(newc))
                omf = float(np.max(base))
                if U > nnf:
                    nnf = U
                if U > omf:
                    omf = U
                if nnf < omf - 1e-11:
                    c[lo:hi + 1] = newc
                    h[p] = float(np.clip(h0 + d, 0.0, 1.0))
                    improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        h = project_sum_box(h, n)
        cc = compute_c5(h, n)
        if cc < bc:
            bc = cc
            bh = h.copy()
    h = project_sum_box(h, n)
    cc = compute_c5(h, n)
    if cc < bc:
        bc = cc
        bh = h.copy()
    return bh, bc

def active_descent(h, n, iters, deadline=None, rng=None, topk=4, lr=0.05, noise=1e-4):
    h = project_sum_box(h, n).copy()
    bh = h.copy()
    bc = compute_c5(h, n)
    s = get_sign(n)
    O = n - 1
    g = None
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    while it < iters:
        if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if g is None or it % 40 == 0:
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            cm = float(np.max(c))
            a = np.where(c >= cm - max(1e-8, 1e-6 * cm))[0]
            if a.size > topk:
                am = int(np.argmax(c))
                if rng is not None and topk > 1:
                    o = rng.choice(a, min(topk - 1, a.size - 1), replace=False)
                else:
                    o = a[:max(0, topk - 1)]
                a = np.unique(np.concatenate(([am], o)))
            gp = np.zeros(n)
            for m in a:
                d = s * (int(m) - O)
                if d >= 0:
                    gp[:n - d] += 1.0 - h[d:]
                    gp[d:] -= h[:n - d]
                else:
                    e = -d
                    gp[e:] += 1.0 - h[:n - e]
                    gp[:n - e] -= h[e:]
            if a.size > 1:
                gp /= float(a.size)
            g = gp
        z = h - lr * g
        if noise > 0.0 and rng is not None:
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
        hp = project_sum_box(z, n)
        nc = compute_c5(hp, n)
        if nc < bc - 1e-13:
            h = hp
            bh = h.copy()
            bc = nc
            stall = 0
            lr = min(0.25, lr * 1.05)
        else:
            lr *= 0.75
            stall += 1
            if lr < 1e-5 or stall > 50:
                break
            if stall % 10 == 0:
                g = None
        it += 1
    return bh, bc

def binary_hill(h, n, iters, deadline=None, rng=None):
    h = np.where(np.round(h) > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(2 * n):
       if abs(h.sum() - target) <= 1e-12:
            break
       if h.sum() < target:
           z = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
           if z.size:
               h[int(rng.choice(z))] = 1.0
       else:
           z = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
           if z.size:
               h[int(rng.choice(z))] = 0.0
    pre = get_precomp(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c)) * 2.0 / n
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    no_improve = 0
    for _ in range(iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        p1 = int(rng.choice(ones))
        p2 = int(rng.choice(zeros))
        nh = h.copy()
        nc = c.copy()
        apply_coord(nh, nc, pre, p1, -1.0)
        apply_coord(nh, nc, pre, p2, 1.0)
        nv = float(np.max(nc))
        if nv < cur - 1e-11:
            h = nh
            c = nc
            cur = nv
            cv = nv * 2.0 / n
            if cv < best_c:
                best_c = cv
                best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        elif nv <= cur + 1e-11 and rng.random_sample() < 0.12:
            h = nh
            c = nc
            cur = nv
            no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > max(800, iters // 2):
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def resample_area(h, n_dest):
    n_src = len(h)
    if n_dest == n_src:
        return project_sum_box(h, n_dest)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_src + 1)
    area = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(h) * (2.0 / n_src)))
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_dest + 1)
    na = np.interp(x1, x0, area)
    hh = (na[1:] - na[:-1]) / (2.0 / n_dest)
    return project_sum_box(hh, n_dest)

def make_seed(n, kind, rng, parent=None):
    kind %= 4
    if kind == 0:
        h = np.zeros(n)
        cnt = int(round(0.5 * n))
        idx = rng.choice(n, cnt, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    elif kind == 1:
        x = 2.0 * (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
        h = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(np.pi * x * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5) + rng.uniform(-3, 3))
        h += 0.15 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5) + rng.uniform(-3, 3))
    elif kind == 2:
        h = np.full(n, rng.uniform(0.0, 0.25))
        for _ in range(int(rng.randint(2, 6))):
            a = int(rng.randint(0, n))
            b = min(n, a + int(rng.randint(1, max(2, n // 5))))
            h[a:b] = 1.0
    else:
        h = 0.5 + rng.uniform(-0.4, 0.4, n)
    return project_sum_box(h, n)

def consider(h, pool, bh, bc, tag):
    n = len(h)
    h = project_sum_box(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    pool.append((c, tag, n, h.copy()))
    if c < bc:
        bh, bc = h.copy(), c
    return bh, bc

def prune_pool(pool, keep):
    if len(pool) <= keep:
        return pool
    p = list(pool)
    out = []
    for _ in range(keep):
        mi = 0
        mc = 1e100
        for i in range(len(p)):
            if p[i][0] < mc:
                mc = p[i][0]
                mi = i
        out.append(p.pop(mi))
    return out

def top_pool(pool, k):
    p = list(pool)
    out = []
    for _ in range(k):
        if not p:
            break
        mi = 0
        mc = 1e100
        for i in range(len(p)):
            if p[i][0] < mc:
                mc = p[i][0]
                mi = i
        out.append(p.pop(mi))
    return out

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    T = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + T - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        parent = np.full(600, 0.5)
    if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size < 2:
        parent = np.full(600, 0.5)
    parent = project_sum_box(parent, parent.size)

    pool = []
    bh = parent.copy()
    bc = 1.0

    bh, bc = consider(parent, pool, bh, bc, 'parent')
    if parent.size > 1:
        bh, bc = consider(parent[::-1].copy(), pool, bh, bc, 'rev')
        bs = 0.5 * (parent + parent[::-1])
        bh, bc = consider(bs, pool, bh, bc, 'sym')

    pdl = t0 + max(30.0, 0.10 * T)
    h1, c1 = cd_optimize(bh, len(bh), 160, pdl, rng, True)
    bh, bc = consider(h1, pool, bh, bc, 'parent_cd')

    ld = t0 + 0.30 * T
    for ns in (32, 48, 64, 80, 104, 128, 160):
        if time.time() > ld:
            break
        for k in range(3):
            if time.time() > ld:
                break
            if k == 0:
                hs = resample_area(bh, ns)
                tag = 'p%d' % ns
            else:
                hs = make_seed(ns, (k + int(seed) + ns) % 4, rng, bh)
                tag = 'r%d%d' % (ns, k)
            hb, cb = binary_hill(hs, ns, max(500, 18000 // ns), ld, rng)
            passes = max(40, min(320, 20000 // ns))
            hb, cb = cd_optimize(hb, ns, passes, ld, rng, True)
            bh, bc = consider(hb, pool, bh, bc, tag + 'cd')
            if time.time() < ld - 5:
                ha, ca = active_descent(hb, ns, min(350, 22000 // ns), ld, rng, 3, 0.04)
                bh, bc = consider(ha, pool, bh, bc, tag + 'ad')
            pool = prune_pool(pool, 10)

    rd = t0 + 0.70 * T
    for nt in (120, 160, 200, 256, 320, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900):
        if time.time() > rd:
            break
        pool = prune_pool(pool, 8)
        new_pool = []
        for cv, tg, nsr, hsr in top_pool(pool, 3):
            if time.time() > rd - 5:
                break
            h0 = resample_area(hsr, nt) if hsr.size != nt else hsr.copy()
            passes = max(25, min(130, 15000 // nt))
            hb, cb = cd_optimize(h0, nt, passes, rd, rng, True)
            if time.time() < rd - 10:
                ha, ca = active_descent(hb, nt, min(160, 25000 // nt), rd, rng, 3, 0.03)
                if ca < cb:
                    hb, cb = ha, ca
            bh, bc = consider(hb, new_pool, bh, bc, 'ref%d_%s' % (nt, tg))
        if new_pool:
            pool = prune_pool(new_pool + pool, 10)

    fp = top_pool(pool, 3)
    if time.time() < deadline - 70:
        for cv, tg, nsr, hsr in fp:
            if time.time() > deadline - 35:
                break
            nf = nsr if nsr <= 850 else 720
            h0 = resample_area(hsr, nf) if hsr.size != nf else hsr.copy()
            passes = 260 if nf <= 400 else 130
            hb, cb = cd_optimize(h0, nf, passes, deadline - 20, rng, False)
            bh, bc = consider(hb, pool, bh, bc, 'fin_' + tg)

    if time.time() < deadline - 50:
        n = len(bh)
        for s in (0.01, 0.004):
            if time.time() > deadline - 25:
                break
            hp = project_sum_box(bh + rng.normal(0.0, s, n), n)
            hb, cb = cd_optimize(hp, n, 140, deadline - 12, rng, True)
            bh, bc = consider(hb, pool, bh, bc, 'pert')

    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
        n = len(bh)
        hb, cb = cd_optimize(bh, n, 800, deadline - 3, rng, False)
        bh, bc = consider(hb, pool, bh, bc, 'final_cd')
        if time.time() < deadline - 3:
            hb, cb = cd_optimize(hb, n, 350, deadline - 1, rng, True)
            bh, bc = consider(hb, pool, bh, bc, 'final_cd2')

    bh = project_sum_box(bh, len(bh))
    bc = compute_c5(bh, len(bh))
    return bh, float(bc), int(len(bh))
```
